# Site catalog of 인천각 (https://incheon.webforai.kr). One line = one page of this site.
# Line letter: P place, G guide, C course, E event series, A foreign article (on /abroad/), V video topic page, T theme list, S site page. The second field is the page path (an article: its id): pass it to get_pages as is, and link the page as https://incheon.webforai.kr + path. A name in brackets is the place's English name; '다른 이름' = other names of the same place (a building and the museum in it, an old and a new name).
# Place lines carry the key facts exactly as the page's first screen shows them, in this order — attraction: 시간 (hours), 휴무 (closed days), 요금 (fee), 주차 (parking), 전화 (phone); food and 카페·디저트 (cafés, tea houses, bakeries — their own list, apart from 음식점): 시간, 휴무, 메뉴 (signature dishes), 주차, 전화; 쇼핑 (markets and shops): 시간, 휴무, 주차, 전화; stay: 체크인, 체크아웃, 주차, 객실 (rooms), 전화. One of these missing from a line = the site has no value for it. '외' = the page has more. '두 출처 다름' = two sources give different values and the page shows both (방문 전 전화 확인). '통합권 N곳' = the places a combined ticket in the fee covers, as the guides name them ('A(=B)': A is the name the guides use, B the place's page name). 지정 = official designations the page lists. Everything else (all facts with sources, FAQ, nearby public parking and bus stops, guide text, event programmes) comes from get_pages.
# 공식 확인처 (official places to confirm a value the site lacks): 인천투어 https://itour.incheon.go.kr/ (인천광역시 공식 관광 안내); 인천관광공사 https://www.ito.or.kr/ (공식 누리집)
